    2023 was a challenging year with weaker than expected post-Covid        二零二三年是充滿挑戰的一年，新冠疫情後
    recovery of global economies, geopolitical tensions and high-interest   全球經濟復蘇弱於預期，地緣政治局勢緊張
    rate in the USD.                                                        及美元利率高企。

    results and showed extraordinary development resilience, mainly         異，顯 示 出 非 凡 的 發 展 韌 性，主 要 得 益 於 本
    due to the Group’s development strategies and the commitment            集團的發展戰略及我們團隊的敬業奉獻。本
    of our dedicated team. Turnover and net profit of the Group were        集 團 的 營 業 額 及 純 利 分 別 為 約 人 民 幣 280 億
    approximately RMB28.0 billion and RMB3.1 billion respectively.          元及人民幣 31 億元。

Dividend Policy                                                            股息政策
Based on our strong cash flow and low gearing ratio, we would like         基 於 我 們 強 勁 的 現 金 流 及 低 資 產 負 債 率，
to share the results with our Shareholders and set a dividend payout       我們希望與股東分享成果，將派息率設定為
ratio of 35+, in which 35% is the basic dividend payout and plus is        35+，其中 35% 為基本派息率，外加特別派息
the special dividend payout (we paid special dividends of 5% in 2022       率（我們於二零二二年及二零二三年分別支付
and 2023 respectively).                                                    5% 的特別股息）。

    With a diversified product development plan, the business structure     本集團通過多元化產品開發規劃，建立了多

     The Board acknowledges that an effective system of internal control       董事會明白有效的內部控制系統及風險管理

     The four key steps in the risk management process are:                      風險管理程序中的四個關鍵步驟如下：

     •      Risk identification and assessment – identify the key risks          •   風險識別及評估－識別本集團的主要風
            of the Group and analyse the risk by considering the possibility         險，並透過考慮事件發生的可能性及對
            of occurrence and the impact to the Group;                               本集團的影響分析有關風險；

     •      Risk handling – adopt an appropriate risk management                 •   風險處理－就每項關鍵風險採取適當的
            strategy (i.e. risk response) for each key risk;                         風險管理策略（即風險回應）；

     •      Risk monitoring – apply monitoring mechanism to ensure               •   風 險 監 察－採 用 監 察 機 制，確 保 風 險 回
            the risk response are operated effectively;                              應行之有效；

     •      Risk reporting – summarise the result of risk assessment             •   風 險 匯 報－概 述 風 險 評 估 結 果，並 向 風
            and report to the Risk Management Team.                                  險管理小組匯報。

     In the risk management process, the top risks within the Group              在風險管理過程中會識別及評估本集團內部
     are identified and assessed; and the respective risk management             的 重 大 風 險，隨 後 管 理 層 會 討 論、協 定 及 實
     measures as well as the corresponding mitigating controls are               施相關風險管理措施和相應的紓緩控制，並
     discussed, agreed and implemented by the management. Risk                   由風險管理小組每年向董事會及審計委員會
     assessment results are reported by the Risk Management Team to              匯報有關風險評估結果。
     the Board and Audit Committee annually.

     Stable Supply of Raw Materials                                             原材料穩定供應
     The major raw material of the Group is corn. Being an agricultural         本集團的主要原材料為玉米。作為農產品，
     product, the plantation and supply of corn may be affected by the          玉米的種植和供應可能會受到天氣或意外事
     weather or unexpected events. If the stable supply of corn is affected     件的影響。倘玉米的穩定供應受到惡劣天氣
     by bad weather or unexpected events, the price of corn, which in turn      或意外事件的影響，玉米價格可能會上漲，
     our cost of production, may increase.                                      進而增加我們的生產成本。

Pre-emptive Rights                                                         優先購買權
There are no provisions for pre-emptive rights under the articles of the   本公司組織章程細則及開曼群島公司法項下
association of the Company and the Companies Law of the Cayman             並無有關優先購買權的條文。
Islands.


Tax Relief and Exemption                                                   稅務減免
Except for dividend income which is exempt from tax in Hong Kong,          除股息收入在香港為免稅外，董事並不知悉
the Directors are not aware of any tax relief and exemption available      股東因持有股份而可享有任何稅務減免。
to the Shareholders by reason of holding the Shares.


Dividend Policy                                                            股息政策
The Board may declare dividend from time to time. The Board has            董事會可不時宣派股息。董事會於考慮以下
absolute discretion in the declaration of dividend after taking into       因素後全權酌情決定股息之宣派：
account the following factors:

1.    Reserves of the Group available for distribution;                    1.   本集團可供分派之儲備；
2.    Performance of the Group;                                            2.   本集團之表現；
3.    Anticipation of future outlook of the economy;                       3.   預測未來經濟前景；
4.    Liquidity position and capital requirements of the Group; and        4.   本集團流動性狀況及資本要求；及
5.    Any other matters considered appropriate by the Board.               5.   董事會認為合適之任何其他事宜。

The listing of the Shares on the Main Board of the Stock Exchange          股份於二零零七年二月八日在聯交所主板上

that the Company has complied with the code provisions as set out          管治守則所載的守則條文。
in the CG Code since then.

     1. GENERAL INFORMATION                                                 1. 一般資料
        Fufeng Group Limited and its subsidiaries manufacture and sell       阜豐集團有限公司及其附屬公司從事製
        fermentation-based food additives, biochemical products and          造及銷售發酵食品添加劑、生化產品及
        starch-based products. The Group has manufacturing plants in         澱粉產品。本集團於中國山東省、陝西省、
        Shandong Province, Shaanxi Province, Heilongjiang Province,          黑龍江省、內蒙古自治區及新疆維吾爾
        Inner Mongolia Autonomous Region and Xinjiang Uygur                  自治區設有生產廠房，主要向中國客戶
        Autonomous Region of the PRC and sells mainly to customers           進行銷售。
        located in the PRC.

        The Company is a limited liability company incorporated in           本公司為於開曼群島註冊成立的有限公

        3.2 Capital management                                                  3.2 資本管理
            (a)   Risk management                                                 (a)   風險管理
                  The Group’s objectives when managing capital are                      本集團的資本管理目標為
                  to

                  •      safeguard their ability to continue as a going                 •   保障其按持續經營基準繼
                         concern, so that they can continue to provide                      續營運的能力，因此彼等
                         returns for shareholders and benefits for other                    可繼續為股東帶來回報，
                         stakeholders, and                                                  同時兼顧其他持份者的利
                                                                                            益，及

                  •      maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce                •   維持最佳資本結構以減低
                         the cost of capital.                                               資金成本。

                  In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure,                 為維持或調整資本架構，本集
                  the Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid                     團或會調整支付予股東的股息
                  to shareholders, return capital to shareholders, issue                金 額、向 股 東 發 還 資 本、發 行
                  new shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                             新股或出售資產以減低債務。

                  The Group monitors capital on the basis of the gearing                本集團根據資產負債比率監察
                  ratio. This ratio is equal to total debt divided by total             其資本。該比率相等於債務總
                  assets at the end of corresponding year. Total debt                   額除以上各對應年度年結日資
                  includes current and non-current bank borrowings                      產總值。於二零二三年十二月
                  and non-current borrowings from third parties as at                   三十一日，債務總額包括即期
                  31 December 2023.                                                     及非即期銀行借貸以及來自第
                                                                                        三方的非即期借貸。

                  The Group’s strategy is to maintain the gearing                       本集團的策略為將資產負債比
                  ratio below 40% (2022: 40%).The gearing ratios at 31                  率 維 持 於 40% 以 下（二 零 二 二

4. CRITICAL ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES AND                                  4. 重要會計估計及判斷
   JUDGMENTS
  The preparation of financial statements requires the use of          財務報表之編製需要使用會計估計，根
  accounting estimates which, by definition, will seldom equal the     據定義，該等會計估計很少會與實際結
  actual results. Management also needs to exercise judgment in        果相同。管理層亦需在應用本集團的會
  applying the Group’s accounting policies.                            計政策時作出判斷。

  Estimates and judgments are continually evaluated and are            對估計及判斷作出持續評估，並以過往
  based on historical experience and other factors, including          經驗及其他因素作基礎，包括在目前情
  expectations of future events that are believed to be reasonable     況下對未來事件的合理預測。
  under the circumstances.

  The Group makes estimates and assumptions concerning the             本集團對未來作出估計及假設。所得會
  future. The resulting accounting estimates will, by definition,      計估計一如其定義甚少與相關的實際結
  seldom equal the related actual results. The estimates and           果相同。以下所述的估計及假設有相當
  assumptions that have a significant risk of causing a material       大風險會引致須於下一個財政年度對資
  adjustment to the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities         產及負債的賬面值作出重大調整。
  within the next financial year are described below.

  4.1 Provision for impairment of trade and other                      4.1 貿易及其他應收款項減值撥備
      receivables
       The loss allowances for financial assets are based on               金融資產的虧損撥備基於對違約風
       assumptions about risk of default and expected loss rates.          險及預期虧損率的假設。本集團根
       The Group uses judgments in making these assumptions                據本集團的過往歷史、現有市場狀
       and selecting the inputs to the impairment calculation,             況以及各報告期末的前瞻性估計，
       based on the Group’s past history, existing market                  使用判斷作出該等假設及選擇減值
       conditions as well as forward looking estimates at the end          計算的輸入數據。所用的主要假設
       of each reporting period. Details of the key assumptions            及輸入數據的詳情於附註 3.1(b) 披露。
       and inputs used are disclosed in Note 3.1(b).

  4.2 Estimated impairment of property, plant and                      4.2 物業、廠房及設備的估計減值
      equipment
       The Group reviews property, plant and equipment for                 倘有事件或情況變動顯示賬面值可
       impairment whenever events or changes in circumstances              能不可收回時，本集團會檢討物業、
       indicate that the carrying amount may not be recoverable.           廠房及設備的減值。現金產生單位
       The recoverable amount of cash-generating unit has been             的可收回金額已按使用價值與公平
       determined based on the higher of value in use and fair             值減銷售成本的較高者釐定。已減
       value less costs to sell. Property, plant and equipment that        值的物業、廠房及設備於各報告日
       suffered an impairment are reviewed for possible reversal of        期檢討是否可能撥回減值。
       the impairment at each reporting date.

            during the Year, in this case 2.43% (2022: nil).                 而言為 2.43%（二零二二年：無）。

  (d) Impairment loss                                                    (d) 減值虧損
       Management judgment is required in the area of asset                 管理層須判斷資產減值範疇，尤其
       impairment particularly in assessing: (i) whether an event           是 評 估：(i) 是 否 已 發 生 可 能 顯 示 有
       has occurred that may indicate that the related assets               關 資 產 價 值 或 不 可 收 回 的 事 件；(ii)
       values may not be recoverable; (ii) whether the carrying             可 收 回 金 額（即 公 平 值 減 處 置 成 本
       value of an asset can be supported by the recoverable                或按於業務中持續使用資產估計的
       amount, being the higher of fair value less costs of                 未來現金流量淨現值兩者中的較高
       disposal or net present value of future cash flows which             者）能 否 支 持 該 項 資 產 的 賬 面 值；
       are estimated based upon the continued use of the asset              及 (iii) 於編製現金流量預測中應用的
       in the business; and (iii) the appropriate key assumptions           適當主要假設，包括該等現金流量
       to be applied in preparing cash flow projections including           預測是否使用適當貼現率貼現。倘
       whether these cash flow projections are discounted using             管 理 層 用 以 評 估 減 值 的 假 設（包 括
       an appropriate rate. Changing the assumptions selected               現金流量預測中採用的貼現率或增
       by management in assessing impairment, including the                 長 率 假 設）改 變，則 可 能 會 對 減 值
       discount rates or the growth rate assumptions in the cash            測試中的淨現值產生重大影響，因
       flow projections, could materially affect the net present            而影響本集團的財務狀況及經營業
       value in the impairment test and as a result affect the              績。若預計表現及相應未來現金流
       Group’s financial condition and results of operations. If            量預測出現重大不利變動，則可能
       there is a significant adverse change in the projected               須在合併利潤表中扣除減值開支。
       performance and resulting future cash flow projections,              倘有跡象顯示減值虧損可能已減少，
       it may be necessary to take an impairment charge to the              則可收回金額不得超過假設並無確
       consolidated income statement. If there is an indication that        認減值的情況下已折舊歷史成本。
       an impairment loss may have decreased, the recoverable
       amount should not be more than what the depreciated
       historical cost would have been if the impairment had not
       been recognised.

28. DEFERRED INCOME TAX                                                  28. 遞延所得稅
  Deferred income tax assets and liabilities are offset when there         當具有將即期所得稅資產與即期所得稅
  is a legally enforceable right to offset current income tax assets       負債抵銷的合法強制執行權，以及當遞
  against current income tax liabilities and when the deferred             延所得稅資產及負債與同一稅務機關就
  income tax assets and liabilities relate to income taxed levied          該應課稅實體或不同應課稅實體徵收的
  by the same taxation authority on either the taxable entity or           所得稅有關，而有關方面擬按淨額基準
  different taxable entities where there is an intention to settle the     償付結餘時，遞延所得稅資產與負債將
  balances on a net basis. The deferred income tax assets and              予抵銷。遞延所得稅資產及負債如下：

      INFORMATION
       This note provides a list of the significant accounting policies        此附註提供一系列編製此等合併財務報
       adopted in the preparation of these consolidated financial              表採納的主要會計政策。除另有註明外，
       statements. These policies have been consistently applied to            此等政策在所列報的所有年度內貫徹應
       all the years presented, unless otherwise stated. The financial         用。財務報表適用於包含阜豐集團有限
       statements are for the Group consisting of Fufeng Group Limited         公司及其附屬公司的本集團。
       and its subsidiaries.

       38.1 Principles of consolidation and equity                             38.1 合併及權益會計法原則
            accounting
            (i)   Subsidiaries                                                   (i)   附屬公司
                  Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured                  附屬公司指本集團對其具有控
                  entities) over which the Group has control. The Group                制 權 的 所 有 實 體（包 括 結 構 性
                  controls an entity where the Group is exposed to, or                 實 體）。當 本 集 團 因 為 參 與 該
                  has rights to, variable returns from its involvement with            實體而承擔可變回報的風險或
                  the entity and has the ability to affect those returns               享有可變回報的權益，並有能
                  through its power to direct the activities of the entity.            力透過其有權控制該實體的活
                  Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the date on                 動影響此等回報時，本集團即
                  which control is transferred to the Group. They are                  控制該實體。附屬公司在控制
                  deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                    權轉移至本集團之日起全部合
                                                                                       併入賬。附屬公司在控制權終
                                                                                       止之日起停止合併入賬。

                  Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised                  集團內公司間交易、結餘及本
                  gains on transactions between Group companies                        集團旗下公司之間的交易未變
                  are eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated                現收益均予以抵銷。未變現虧
                  unless the transaction provides evidence of an                       損亦會抵銷，惟有關交易提供
                  impairment of the transferred asset. Accounting                      證據顯示所轉讓資產出現減值，
                  policies of subsidiaries have been changed where                     則另當別論。如有需要會更改
                  necessary to ensure consistency with the policies                    附屬公司的會計政策，以確保
                  adopted by the Group.                                                與本集團採用的政策保持一致。

38. SUMMARY OF OTHER POTENTIALLY                                        38. 其他可能重大的會計政策資料
    MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                            摘要（續）
    INFORMATION (Continued)
  38.1 Principles of consolidation and equity                            38.1 合併及權益會計法原則（續）
       accounting (Continued)
      (ii)   Associates                                                      (ii)   聯營公司
             Associates are all entities over which the Group                       聯營公司指所有本集團對其有
             has significant influence but not control or joint                     重大影響力而無控制或共同控
             control. This is generally the case where the Group                    制權的實體。此種情況本集團
             holds between 20% and 50% of the voting rights.                        通常持有 20% 至 50% 投票權的
             Investments in associates are accounted for using                      股權。於聯營公司的投資最初

  38.7 Impairment of non-financial assets                                 38.7 非金融資產減值
      Assets that are subject to amortisation are reviewed for               當出現事件或情況改變顯示未必能
      impairment whenever events or changes in circumstances                 收回賬面值時，須予攤銷的資產會
      indicate that the carrying amount may not be recoverable.              進行減值檢討。減值虧損按資產賬
      An impairment loss is recognised for the amount by which               面值超過其可收回金額的差額確認。
      the asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable                    可收回金額指資產公平值減出售成
      amount. The recoverable amount is the higher of an                     本與使用價值間的較高者。就減值
      asset’s fair value less costs of disposal and value in use.            評估而言，資產按可獨立分辨現金
      For the purposes of assessing impairment, assets are                   流量的最低水平（現金產生單位）分
      grouped at the lowest levels for which there are separately            類。商譽以外的已減值非金融資產，
      identifiable cash flows (cash-generating units). Non-financial         於各報告期末檢討是否可能撥回減
      assets other than goodwill that suffered an impairment are             值。
      reviewed for possible reversal of the impairment at the end
      of each reporting period.

            will either be recorded in profit or loss or OCI. For                  其收益及虧損計入損益或其他

      at fair value. The Group holds the trade receivables with             始確認。本集團持有應收貿易賬款
      the objective to collect the contractual cash flows and               的目的是收取合同現金流量，因此
      therefore measures them subsequently at amortised cost                後續使用實際利率法按攤銷成本計
      using the effective interest method. See Note 20 for further          量應收貿易賬款。有關本集團就應

       38.16 Borrowings                                                       38.16 借貸
           Borrowings are initially recognised at fair value, net of             借貸初步以公平值扣除所產生交易
           transaction costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently               成本確認。借貸其後按攤餘成本列
           carried at amortised cost; any difference between the                 賬；所得款項（扣除交易成本）與贖
           proceeds (net of transaction costs) and the redemption                回價值間的任何差額，乃以實際利
           value is recognised in the consolidated income statement              息法於借貸期間在合併利潤表確認。
           over the period of the borrowings using the effective                 倘很有可能提取部分或全部融資，
           interest method. Fees paid on the establishment of loan               則設立貸款融資時支付的費用確認
           facilities are recognised as transaction costs of the loan to         為貸款的交易成本。在此情況下，
           the extent that it is probable that some or all of the facility       該費用可遞延入賬直至提取貸款為
           will be drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred until           止。如並無證據證明部分或全部融
           the draw-down occurs. To the extent there is no evidence              資很有可能被提取，則該項費用資
           that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be          本化以作為流動資金服務的預付款
           drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a pre-payment for               項，並於有關融資期間攤銷。
           liquidity services and amortised over the period of the
           facility to which it relates.

           Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the           除非本集團有權無條件將債務遞延
           Group has an unconditional right to defer settlement of the           至報告期間結算日後至少十二個月

           period.                                                               負債。

      to the acquisition, construction or production of qualifying          長時間方能達致擬定用途或出售狀
      assets, which are assets that necessarily take a substantial          態 的 資 產）直 接 應 佔 的 一 般 及 特 定
      period of time to get ready for their intended use or sale,           借貸成本，均計入該等資產的成本，
      are added to the cost of those assets, until such time as             直至該等資產基本上達致其擬定用
      the assets are substantially ready for their intended use or          途或出售狀態為止。
      sale.

      Investment income earned on the temporary investment of               在特定借貸撥作合資格資產支出前
      specific borrowings pending their expenditure on qualifying           的暫時投資所賺取的投資收入，須
      assets is deducted from the borrowing costs eligible for              自合資格資本化的借貸成本中扣除。
      capitalisation.

      Other borrowing costs are expensed in the period in which             其他借貸成本於其產生期間費用化。
      they are incurred.

  38.18 Current and deferred income tax                                 38.18 即期及遞延所得稅
      The income tax expense or credit for the period is the tax            期內所得稅開支或抵免指根據各司
      payable on the current period’s taxable income based on               法權區的適用所得稅率按即期應課
      the applicable income tax rate for each jurisdiction adjusted         稅收入支付的稅項，並根據由於暫
      by changes in deferred income tax assets and liabilities              時性差異及未使用稅務虧損而導致
      attributable to temporary differences and to unused tax               的遞延所得稅資產和負債變動作出
      losses.                                                               調整。

      (a)   Current income tax                                              (a)   即期所得稅
            The current income tax charge is calculated on                        即期所得稅支出根據本公司及
            the basis of the tax laws enacted or substantively                    其附屬公司營運及產生應課稅
            enacted at the balance sheet date in the countries                    收入所在國家於資產負債表日
            where the Company and its subsidiaries operate and                    已頒佈或實質頒佈的稅務法例
            generate taxable income. Management periodically                      計算。管理層就適用稅務法例
            evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect to              以詮釋為準的情況定期評估稅
            situations in which applicable tax regulation is subject              項收益狀況，並在適用情況下
            to interpretation. It establishes provisions where                    根據預期須向稅務機關支付的
            appropriate on the basis of amounts expected to be                    稅款設定撥備。
            paid to the tax authorities.

  38.23 Leases                                                          38.23 租賃
      Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a                   在本集團可使用租賃資產之日，租
      corresponding liability at the date at which the leased asset         賃確認為使用權資產及相應負債。
      is available for use by the Group.

      Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially             租賃產生的資產及負債按現值基準
      measured on a present value basis. Lease liabilities                  進行初始計量。租賃負債包括固定
      include the net present value of fixed payments (including            付款額（包括實質固定付款額）扣除
      in-substance fixed payments), less any lease incentives               任何應收的租賃激勵的淨現值。
      receivable.

      The lease payments are discounted using the interest                  租賃付款使用租賃中內含的利率進
      rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily            行貼現。倘無法輕易確定該利率（為
      determined, which is generally the case for leases in the             本 集 團 租 賃 的 一 般 情 況），則 使 用
      Group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used,               承租人的增量借款利率，即個別承
      being the rate that the lessee would have to pay to borrow            租人在類似經濟環境中按類似條款
      the funds necessary to obtain an asset of similar value in            及條件借入獲得與使用權資產具有
      a similar economic environment with similar terms and                 類似價值的資產所需資金而必須支
      conditions.                                                           付的利率。

       38.23 Leases (Continued)                                             38.23 租賃（續）
           To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group:             為釐定增量借款利率，本集團應：

           •    where possible, uses recent third-party financing              •   在可能情況下，以承租人最近
                received by the individual lessee as a starting point,             收到的第三方融資為起點，並
                adjusted to reflect changes in financing conditions                進行調整以反映融資條件自收
                since third party financing was received.                          到第三方融資後的變化。

                Lease payment is allocated between the principal and               租賃付款額於本金及融資費用
                finance cost. The finance cost is charged to profit or             之間作出分配。融資費用在租
                loss over the lease period so as to produce a constant             賃期內計入損益，以按照固定
                periodic rate of interest on the remaining balance of              的周期性利率對各期間負債餘
                the liability for each period.                                     額計算利息。

                Right-of-use assets are measured at cost comprising                使用權資產按成本計量，包括
                the following:                                                     以下各項：

                •    the amount of the initial measurement of lease                •   租賃負債的初始計量金
                     liability, and                                                    額；及

                •    any lease payments made at or before the                      •   在開始日期或之前支付的
                     commencement date.                                                任何租賃付款。

                Right-of-use assets are generally depreciated over the             使用權資產一般於資產的使用
                shorter of the asset’s useful life and the lease term on           壽命與租賃期兩者孰短的期間
                a straight-line basis.                                             按直線法計提折舊。

                Payments associated with short-term leases of                      與短期倉庫租賃相關的付款額
                warehouse are recognised on a straight-line basis as               按直線法於損益中確認為開支。
                an expense in profit or loss. Short-term leases are                短期租賃是指租賃期為 12 個月
                leases with a lease term of 12 months or less.                     或小於 12 個月的租賃。

         multiplied by 365 days.

         by 365 days.
